 [![](https://cusat.ac.in/images/h-about.webp) CUSAT](https://cusat.ac.in/about)

 The birth of the University of Cochin was with the specific purpose of “development of higher education with particular emphasis on post-graduate studies and research in applied science, technology, industry and commerce”. Later reorganized as CUSAT.

 [Read more](https://cusat.ac.in/about)
